1a1(rgyud): @//rdo rje mkha' 'gro'i rgyud bzhugs s+ho////
2a1(rgyud): @##/7/shri badzra DA ka nA ma ma hA tan tra rA dzA/bod 
2a2(rgyud): skad du/rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po chan pa dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro zhes 
2a3(rgyud): bya ba/bcom ldan 'das thugs rje chen po 'phags pa spyan ras   
2b1(rgyud): gzigs dbang phyug la phyag 'tshal lo//mkha' 'gro kun dngos sems dpa' ni//rdo rje mkha' 
2b2(rgyud): 'gro bde ba'i mchog/gsang ba mchog gi dges pa na//thams cad bdag nyid rtag tu bzhugs//
2b3(rgyud): 'di ni rang byung bcom ldan 'das//lhag pa yi ni lha gcig nyid//'byung gnas sgra tshul nges 'byung 
2b4(rgyud): rnams//dam tshig spyod pa'i spyod yul yin//'jig rten gsum du rnyed dka' ba//thog mar dbus 
3a1(rgyud): @#/ O /mthar yang dag gnas//'dod chags ma yin chags bral min//dbu    
3a2(rgyud): mar yang ni mi dmigs so//bud med kun gyi sgyu ma'i rgya//'di ni gnyis med ye shes mchog/rnyed 
3a3(rgyud): dang bde ba kun mos pas//ngan par spyod pa'ang 'grub par 'gyur//nam mkha' kun tu rgyu grub pas//
3a4(rgyud): mkha' 'gro ma zhes rab tu grags//sna tshogs kun tu phyag rgya ni//sna tshogs kun gyis sdom pa yis/
3b1(rgyud): mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba//rdo rje mkha' 'gro zhes byar grub//de nyid med sems rnal 'byor pa//lha 
3b2(rgyud): la dmigs par byed pa dag/kun tu gzugs brnyan nyid kyi ni//lus sogs rnal 'byor skye ba med//byang chub 
3b3(rgyud): sems chen rnal 'byor gang //rnal 'byor dbang phyug rang nyid gtso//byang chub sems 'di rdo rje ste//sangs rgyas kun 
3b4(rgyud): gyi rang bzhin no//'gro ba'i rtsa lag rang bzhin gyis//sna tshogs g.yog te rnam bzhugs pas//de phyir 'bad pa thams 
3b5(rgyud): cad kyis//sna tshogs tshul gyis mchod par bya//bdag nyid sangs rgyas thams cad dang //dpa' bo thams cad nyid yin 
4a1(rgyud): @#/ O /te;;rang gi lha yi sbyor ba yis//bdag nyid rab tu bsgrub par bya//'di yis 
4a2(rgyud): sangs rgyas thams cad dang //dpa' bo thams cad nyid dang ni//rdo rje 'dzin pa thams cad du//tshe 'di nyid du 'grub
4a3(rgyud): 'gyur zhes//mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba'i//rdo rje mkha' 'gro bde ba'i mchog/rdo rje sems dpa' de    
4a4(rgyud): bzhin gshegs//dpal ldan bcom ldan 'das kyi gsungs//thams cad nam mkha'i mtshan nyid de//nam 
4a5(rgyud): mkha' la ni mtshan nyid med//khams gsum pa ni ma lus pa'i//'gro kun sgyu ma lta bu yin//brtan pa dang ni 
4b1(rgyud): g.yo ba yi//'gro ba dmigs su med pa ste//mthong ba dang ni reg pa'i dngos//thams cad sgyu ma ji bzhin no//gang 
4b2(rgyud): gis blun po 'ching 'gyur ba//'dir des mkhas rnams rnam par rol//phyag rgya'i sbyor ba 'di yis ni//sa gsum 
4b3(rgyud): ma lus bza' bar bya//gzhan du gal te sbyor ba ni//ma tshang mar me lta bur 'da'//gzugs ni sangs rgyas rnam snang 
4b4(rgyud): mdzad//tshor ba rdo rje nyi ma yin//'du shes pad ma gar dbang ste//'du byed rdo rje rgyal po yin//rnam shes rdo rje sems   
4b5(rgyud): dpa' ste//'di dag dngos kun rang bzhin ni//rdo rje mkha' 'gro zhes byar grags//yang dag chen po gcig bu'i lha//
4b6(rgyud): bde ba thams cad rab ster ba//sgyu ma thams chen po'i rang bzhin gyis//chags bral chags pa zhes byar bshad//
5a1(rgyud): sa khams ltung byed ma yin te//de bzhin chu khams gsod ma'o//me khams 'gugs byed ma yin te//de bzhIn rlung ni
5a2(rgyud): gar dbang ngo//namakha'i khams ni rgyu ma che/rang nyid 'gro ma zhes byar bshad//sangs rgyas dkun dang mnyam sbyor ba//mkha'agro ma ni 'bar ba'i mchog/
5a3(rgyud): 'byung ba lnga'i rang bzhin gyis//de bzhin rdo rje gtso bor gnas//mig gnyis gti mug rdo rje bshad//nyan pa zhe sdang rdo rjer brjod//sna ni ser
5a4(rgyud): sna rdo rje ste//kha ni 'dod chags rdo rjer gsus//lus ni phra dog rdo rje ste//skye mched thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//rdo rje mkha'agro bde 
5a5(rgyud): ba'i mchog/gzugs 'di thams cad stong pa nyid//stong pa'i tshul gyi mig gis ni//dogs pa med par bsgom pa'i mchog/
5a6(rgyud): rnam par mi rtog pa yis lnga//namakha'i tshul gyis sems gyis ni//der ni thams cad namakha'i dngos//dogs pa med pa'i bsgom pa ldan//
5a7(rgyud): rnam par mi rtog skye 'gyur zhes//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs/bcom ldan rdo rje cad gyis gsungs/de mas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams 
5b1(rgyud): cang thog(sa) pa med cing gdon mi za ba chen po'i dam tshig ces bya ba'i rim (pa)ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhus (pa) nas//'di skad (thog ma) ces gsungaso//ji srid 'khor ba'i ni/mkhas 
5b2(rgyud): mchog 'dug par gyur pa'i ser/de srid sems can mtshungs med don//mya ngan mi mnga' bar mdzad//de ltar sems cad dam pa rnams//srid pa'i zhags pa'i 
5b3(rgyud): 'jigs med par/mi skyo bar mi brtson 'grus kyis//rtags tu sems can don rnams mdzad//namakha'i gnas kyi mthar thugs par//smon lam rnam par 
5b4(rgyud): sbyong cing//sems can don brtsom sesaM (ma)gyis ni//dpa' bo srid pa spyod par mdzad/rtag tu 'dod pas myos pa yi//lha yi bu 'di rnams dang ni/gzhan yang skye bo chag+sa
5b5(rgyud): can phyir/'dod chags thams cad dag par mdzad/bde chen shin du grub bya'i phyir/rtog pa rgyas pa sbyin pa dang/thog ma tha ma med pa'i mchog/rtog pa thams cad dang por bshad/
5b6(rgyud): 'das pa'i dus na masngon byung ba//rtog pa dang po bde ba che/der ni rdo rje sems can gyis//lhag pa yi ni lha gcig bzhugs//'jig rten khams rnams mtha' yas kyi/sems can kun 
5b7(rgyud): don 'byung bya'i phyir//glu dang rol mo sogs pa'i gar//srid pa kun gyi brtso bor gyur//sems can thams cad 'byung ba yi//dga' ba yang dag bsdus ba yang/de nas sems can (skye ) 
5'a1(rgyud): @#/ O /'gyur ba'i//'dod pa'i chags pa rnam spangs te/nges 'dul bar ni mkhyen nasu//yi ge las 'byung rnam spangs shing/namakha'i lta bur bdag med pa/dag ba nyid du de nas 
5'a2(rgyud): son /de nas sems can sangs rgyas kyi//bla med don ni nus med pa'i/stong pa nyid la rtag bzhugs pa'i/'gro kun stong p+wa bcom na(sa) ni/thaMs cad dmyal bar ltung mthong ste//slar yang bdag dang gzhan 
5'a3(rgyud): don la/mos pas gus pa nyis du gyur//de nas 'jig rten khams kun nas//bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas kun sdus te/myur ba nyid du bdag la bskul/oM mahA su kha badzras twa dza hUM baM ho su 
5'a4(rgyud): su ra ta twAM/'dis ni bskun ba tsam gyis kyang/dag pa'i bde ba kun sterab//'dod pa'i don kun grub (kyang) byed cing/'gro kun bde ba bskyed par gyur/nga ni 'dod pa'i gzug+sa kyi mchog /sangs rgyas kun gyis byin
5'a5(rgyud): brlags pa//da nyid chos kyi sku yi mchog/sangs rgyas gyi bdag po yin/sangs rgyas kun gyi sbyor 'phrog cing/sangs rgyas kun gyi bde ba che/sangs rgyas kun gyi sku chen po/sangs rgyas kun kyi gsung 
5'a6(rgyud): bsnyan pa/sangs rgyas kun kyi thugs chen yin//sangs rgyas kun kyi mchod pas che//sangs rgyas kun gyi rgyal po che/rdo rje 'dzin pa kun (gyi)bdag/'jig rten dbang phyug kun gyi gartso//
5'a7(rgyud): rin chen 'dzin pa kun pa kun gyi dbang //mkha'agro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba//sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam sbyor 'gyur//sangs rgyas kun dang (   )mnyam (   )sa na//rdo rje mkha'agro zhes byar 'grub 
5'b1(rgyud): dkyil 'khor chen po'i sbyor ba yis/de nas dkyil 'khor thams cad bri//de yi dbusu tshul bzhin du //rdo rje sems dpa' bzhag par byas/zla ba'i mdog can 'dzum dang bcas/'od zer 
5'b2(rgyud): dmar po 'khrugs pa las/sangs rgyas (phrang) (phrang)sa mdzes pa ste//rgyan kun gyis ni rnam par klubs//zhal bzhi phyag bzhi spyan gsum dang/ral pa'i thor tshugs kyi bca brgyan cing/lha mo bzhi dang 
5'b3(rgyud): yang dag ldan/sangs rgyas gzugs kyis nye bar brgyan//rtsa zhal zhi zhing g.yas dkar yin//de bzhin gzhan yang g.yon pa dmang/rgyab zhal ser po gser bsregs 'ad/thod pa la bzhug+sa 
5'b4(rgyud): gar dang ldan/sum bcu gsum pa 'jigs par mdzad/rdo rje dang ni lja gri dang//dril bu thod pa bsnams pa nyid//mkha'agro ma'i 'khor los gang/skyob pa sna tshogs bri bar bya/mkha'agro kun 
5'b5(rgyud): dang mnyam sbyor ba'i//rdo rje mkha'agro bde mchog zhes/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs/bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs/ma dang sring mo bu mo dang/de bzhin nyid du mo rnams ni/
5'b6(rgyud): dngos grub thams cad rab bsgrub pas//'dod pa'i sbyor bas chags par bya/ba glang ngan dang rta ngan sogs/dogs pa med pa(ra) bza' bya/'jig rten la rtag g.yon la sbyod/brtan pa dang ni g.yo ba yi/
5'b7(rgyud): 'gro kun g.yon las byung bas na/g.yon gi bza' zhing g.yon mchod bya//pho nya ma gtogs gzhan rnams la/'dod pa rmongs pas bgrod mi bya/po nya mo rtag zhen thar par/'gyur ba nyid du the (   ) tshom med/
5'b8(rgyud): bgrub pos mchog du 'bad byas ste//brtul zhugs mi spang brtul zhugs rdzogs//dam tshig rim pa spyod pa dang//tsa ru'i gza' ba lhan (ga)cig bya/rigs lnga la ni kun spyod pa//
6a1(rgyud): @#/ O /rigs cig nyid du rtag par bya//sprang tsi khrag dang ga bur bcas//tsan dan dmar po sbyar byas la//rdo rje chu dang bsres pa ni//dngos 
6a2(rgyud): grub thams cad bsgrub byed pa'i//de dag bdud rtsi lngar ni 'gyur//mthe bong bsrin lag sbyar ba yis//rnal 'byor rig pas rtag tu bza'//zhim pa'i gtung ba 
6a3(rgyud): bzhin myangs na//dngos grub rtag pa thob par 'gyur//'di dag dam tshig dam pa ste//mdzes ma'i gdong can khyod la bshad//las rnams kun la thams bcas 
6a4(rgyud): shis//dog pa med par gang spyod kyang //rnam par mi rtag bsgom pa ni//brtul zhugs rnams kyi mchog gi mchog//las rnams kun la kun du ni//rnam par mi rtog    
6a5(rgyud): bsgom byed cing //dogs pa med par kun tu spyod//de ni dka' thub rnams kyi mchog//the tshom med pa'i sems kyis ni//skra zla la sogs rjes dngos kyi//yul 
6a6(rgyud): rnams ma lus rten byed pa'i//dka' thub shin tu bka' ba'o//gang yang las rnams thams cad la//shes rab dang ni sbyor byas te//de yang stong pa'i gnasu sbyar//
6a7(rgyud): de 'dir bdag chen bka' thub yin//rnam par mi rtog sems kyis ni//shes rab dang ni mtshungs gzugs la//dog(sa) med spyad pa'i kun spyod pa'i//de ni bdag chen    
6a8(rgyud): dka' thub yin//shes rab thabs ni ma gtogs par//gang tshe sems bzhan mi g.yo na//de tshe byang chub chen po ni//nges par ster ba nyidu bshad //rnam par mi rtog 
6b1(rgyud): rjes 'jug la//rnal 'byor pa ni mngon par brtson//de ni thams cad 'byung ba yi//shes rab pha rol phyin pa yin//gang tshe 'dus byas tshul rnams ni//rnam par mi rtog
6b2(rgyud): sems kyis su//me long lta bur rab shes pa//de ni shes rab brten pa yin//glog dang chu bur ltar smin pa//gang tshe 'dus byas tshularnams ni//gang gis ji ltar
6b3(rgyud): mthong 'gyur ba/de ni shes rab rten pa yin/me long nang gi gzugs brnyan dang //rmi lam sgyu ma dri za'i grong //mig 'phrul mtshungs par gang gis mthong //de ni   
6b4(rgyud): gtso bo yin par brjod//de ni gang du 'ang sngon zhen dang //gang du'ang gnas pa yod ma yin//de ltar yang dag 'byung mod kyi//sgyu ma lta bu'i dngos dag ni//long spyod   
6b5(rgyud): khyed 'phag+sa skye 'gyur ba//bde ba yongs su 'gyur bzhing bas//'bras bu khyad 'phags thob 'gyur ba//ci'i phyir ni 'dod mi bya//brtan g.yo'i dngos po yod min te/
6b6(rgyud): 'on kyang snang ba tsam mtshan nyid//bsgom pa khyad par can gang yin//goms pa'i stobs ni rdzogs pa na//yid 'ong bde ba'i 'brus ni//bla med gsal ba blo yi mthu//
6b7(rgyud): 'bras bu don gnyer rgyu bsgrubs na/der ni lta mo su zhig 'gog//de bas 'bad pa thams cad kyis//kun du 'gro ma mchod par bya//bka' thub sdom pa mi zad pas//
6b8(rgyud): lus ni skams shing sdug bsngal 'gyur//sdug bsngal gyis ni sems g.yengs na//g.yengs bas dngos grub gzhan du 'gyur//lus dang sems ni brtan gyur na/
6'a1(rgyud): @#/ O /bde ba thams cad brtan par 'gyur/sdug bsngal gyis ni g.yo gyur pas//shin tu 'gog par 'go par 'gyur//des na lus dang ngag sems rnamam//brtan 
6'a2(rgyud): par byas na byang chub thob//gzhan yang dus min 'chi ba dang //dmyal bar nges par bsreg 'gyur zhes//mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba'i //rdo rje mkha'agro bde ba'i mchog//
6'a3(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs//rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro l+sa//de kho na nyid snang ba dam pa'i yul la    
6'a4(rgyud): 'jug pa'i ye shes zhes bya ba ste//rim pa phye ba dang po'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das la/btsun mo rnams kyis slar yang mchod cing phyag byas     
6'a5(rgyud): nas//'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das snying po gang gis/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bstan pa 'bral ba'i lha la sogs pa'i gdug pa can rnams dang /
6'a6(rgyud): skye bo gdug pa can gzhan dag dang /'joms pa dang /zhum par byed pa dang /ltung bar zhi bar bya te/byang chub kyi snying po'i bar du gnod pa byed par mi 'gyur ba'i/snying po 
6'a7(rgyud): sdang ba thams cad rab tu 'joms pa lha'i bshad du gsol//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje'i bdag pos glu  
6'a8(rgyud): blangs te 'di skad gsungaso//dang po rnam par snang mdzad gnas//der ni sde tshan rnam+sa kyi mtha'//ta dang pa'i ya d+ha b+ha'i mtha'//dbyangs kyi gsuM pas spyir brgyan pa'i//sa 
6'b1(rgyud): yi mtha' ni rang gis lta/la sa'i dbus kyi stan zhugs pa'i//sa yi mtha' ni sbyar bar bya//mda' gnyis kyis ni rnam par sbyang //a stan gnas pa sa yi mtha'//slar yangs 
6'b2(rgyud): mtha' mnga' bstan//zla ba'i dum bu thig les brgyan//la sa'i dbus gnas sa bon ni//mnga' bcu yis ni yongasu sbyangs//sa dang sa'i dbus gnas la//zhal gsuM 
6'b3(rgyud): gyis ni brgyan pa dang //rlung gi phyogs gnas sa bon la/rutra yis ni rnam par brgyan//hUM hUM ha ni bzung nas su//dbyangs kyi dgu pas yang dag dag//phaT ces 
6'b4(rgyud): bya ba dgod par bya/lhag ma ha yi rkang ba la//lnga ma'i dbus gnas de nyid te//de la ha dza zhes bya'o//ra stan la gnas pa yi mtha'//i zhes bya ba'i mda' dang 
6'b5(rgyud): sbyar//slar yang ba zhes bya ba'i mtha'//mda' ni slar bas yongasu sbyang+sa //sa mtha' der ni ra zhig zhon//e zhes bya ba rna ba'i brgyan//i dbyangs zla dum thig 
6'b6(rgyud): les brgyan//yi ge ra zhon pa yi mtha'//slar yang mtha' sgra lngar ldan/zla du thig les cod ban brgyan//slar yang de nyid mgon po bzla//rin chen mtha' ma phaT 
6'b7(rgyud): kyis brgyan//rnam par rnang mdzad sna tshogs kyis//tshig rnams kun la dgang bya zhes/dpa' bo rtag dang mnyam sbyor ba'i//mkha' 'gro ma yi dra ba'i sdom//rdo rje   
6'b8(rgyud): sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs//dpa' bo rtug dang mnyam sbyor ba'i/mkha' 'gro ma yi dra ba'i sdi+om//sna tshogs pad ma'i 
7a1(rgyud): @#/ O /nyi dkyil la//der ni hUM las byung ba yi//las kyi rdo rje'i dbus gnas par //hUM gi de nyid rnam bsgom pa'o//'od zer sna tshogs 'bar ba las//
7a2(rgyud): mkha' 'gro ma'i 'khor spro ba'o//zhal cig phyag gnyis spyan gsum pa//sku mdog dmar po drag chen mo//mdung gi dbyibs can 'jigs pa'i gzugs//spyan dmar   
7a3(rgyud): kham skra gyen du mdzes//lang tsho phun sum tshogs par ldan//stag gi spags pa'i nam bza' can//ljags 'dril bas ni cher 'jigs te//'jigs pa dag kyang     
7a4(rgyud): 'jigs par mdzad//mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba//rdo rje mkha'agro rab grags zhes//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gsheg+sa//bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsung+sa //
7a5(rgyud): sngon bsags las ni mthar byed pa//me dang bcas te lan gnyis bzla//ku ru ku ru sems dpa' bral//ral pa gya gyur ban da gsal//de ni slar yang bzlas 
7a6(rgyud): par bya//'jig rten gzugs dang bral ba yi//tra sa ya zhes bya ste brdzun//dam pa de ni lan gnyis bzla//kra sha zhes pa oM sgrar ldan//glo na b+ha ya yang dag gnas//
7a7(rgyud): de yang slar ni bzlas par bya//ma'i mtha' ni nyi stan gnas//mnga' bcu zla dum thig les dgang //de yang slar ni brjod par bya//'od byed sbyangs pa yi ni mtha'//
7a8(rgyud): gshegs su gsol ba glo na gnas//nyis 'gyur du ni bya ba yin//zla zhon thig gsal phe zhes bya//de yang slar ni bzlas par bya//phaT ces bya ba bgyings te bsgrag+sa//
7b1(rgyud): rnam par rtog pa kun gyi shing //da ha da ha'i mkha' 'gro'i sdom//lnga sde'i thog ma gnyis dang ldan//sde gtso de'i slar bzlas bya//bak+sha bak+sha zhes bya dang //ba sa ru 
7b2(rgyud): hati ra an tra//ma la b+ha la pi ne dang //gr-ih+Na gr-ih+Na sab ta ste//pa ta la g+ha ta pu dza g+haM//sa paM ba tang tsa ya nyis 'gyur bya//a dang ka'i yi ge dang //gsum pa 
7b3(rgyud): yi ni gsum par sbyar//bzhi pa de ni rlung la gnas//lan gnyis su ni bzlas par bya//sa yi mtha' ni nyi mar gnas//yi ge bzhi pas dgang bar bya//slar 
7b4(rgyud): yang de ni lan gnyis bzla//lnga sde mtha' yi stan bzhugs pa//ka sha a dang thig ler ldan//sa bon de ni gnyis bzlas bya//sa mtha' mda' gnyis dang ldan pa//
7b5(rgyud): slar yang sa mtha' mda' gsum sbyar//de bzhin sa mtha' mda' lnga'i stan//de kun zla ba'i dum bu dang //thig le yis ni cod pan brgyan//de dag yang yang 
7b6(rgyud): bsgyur bar bya//ka sa tsa dang da rnams ni//i sgra yi ni mchog gis gzung //li yi mtha' ni re re sbyar//de dag kyang ni yang yang bsgyur//u sgra la zhon sa yi mtha'/
7b7(rgyud): slar yang zla dum thig les sbyangs//phaT ces bya ba'i rin chen bsgrub//chang dang (ma)min sna tshogs pa'i//me tog mang po yang dag zin//de dag lha mo sgyu ma che//dngos grub 
7b8(rgyud): thams cad rab ster ba'o//'di ni gsungs pa tsam gyis kyang //'jig rten khams kun g.yos gyur cing //de bzhin gshegs pa kun bskrag nas//slar yang de bzhin 
8a1(rgyud): @#/ O /gshegs pa kun//byang chub tu ni mngon sangs rgyas//ri bo thams cad 'dar zhing gas//ma mo'i tshogs dang 'byung po'i tshogs//
8a2(rgyud): gdon dang rims dang dbyig dug dang //gnod sbyin srin po log 'dren tshogs//sdang ba thams cad btul zhing bcom//zhi 'gyur bkug ste dkyil 'khor gyi//
8a3(rgyud): dbus su bcug ste dam tshig gis//bcings nas kyang ni dbang du mdzad//dmyal ba ya ni sdig pa kun//dral te nges bcom bsnyil mo zhes//mkha' 'gro kun dang 
8a4(rgyud): mnyam sbyor ba'i//rdo rje mkha' 'gro bde ba'i mchog/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs//gru bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa//
8a5(rgyud): sgo skyong dang ni yang dag ldan//de dbus pad ma bri bya ba//'od zer dmar chen gsal ba'i mdangs//phyi rol ri mo bzlum pos bskor//de yi dbus su bcom ldan 'das//
8a6(rgyud): khro bo chen po zhal bzhis brgyan//zhal gyi mche ba bcung zad brtsigs//khro dang bcas shing bshad pa'i zhal//g.yas pa dkar zhing zhi ba ste//g.yon pa dmar po 
8a7(rgyud): cher 'jigs pa//rgyab ni 'od zer ser po'i mdog/phyag bzhi yis ni brgyan pa ste/dpa' bo g.yon na kha T+wAM dang//de bzhin du ni thong pa snams//g.yas na 'bar ba'i rdo rje dang//
8a8(rgyud): khyab par du ni shor gyis brgyan//rig ma bzhi dang yang dag ldan/sangs rgyas gzugs kyis nye bar brgyan//dpa' bo'i shes rab dga' mdzes ma//mda' dang zhug ni bsnams
8b1(rgyud): pa ste//sems dpa' sngon po 'bar ba'i dngos//'od kyi dkyil 'khor dmar po can/dngu khrod me ltar gsad mthong zhing//'jigs pa yi ni dus ltar drag/me ltar 'bar ba'i /
8b2(rgyud): skra la ni//thod pa yi ni dbu brgyan cing//ro langs dang ni lhan cig rlon/he ru dka'i gar kyis spyod/ma mo'i grong khyor nyams dga' bar//bas yi dbang phyug rol par mdzad//
8b3(rgyud): thams cad du ni thams cad kyis/kun tu thams cad kun bdag nyid//mkha' gro kun dang mnyas (ma)ba'i//rdo rje mkha' 'gro bde ba'i mchog/bsam gtan bzlas pa'i sbyor
8b4(rgyud): ba yis//'gru' 'gyur 'di ni the tshom med//dus bzhi ru ni rab sbyor bas/bdo mchog blaM sgom bya ste/rdo rje chen po 'bum bzlas na/khro bo'i rgyal po mtshungs par 'gyur/rdo rje che 'dzin 
8b5(rgyud): tshe dang ni//mdog dang na so stobs dang brtsal/dngos grub rdzu 'phrul chen por ldan/mi zad 'gro don byed mthun 'gyur//yang na ma bsgrub par yang sngags pas lan gcig bzlas//
8b6(rgyud): las rgya tha dam pa byed par 'gyur ba ni//'di lta ste/sgyu ma dang ni sgyu ma thams cad ngos par gnon pa dang//ltung ba dang/bskyed pa dang//skrag pa dang//rmug pa dang /rengs pa dang//
8b7(rgyud): bzlog pa dang//rmongs pa dang//bcing pa dang/dgas pa dang/gtor ba dang/brlag par bya ba dang//basmyo bar bya ba dang//gtub pa dang/phyug pa dang//bye ba dang/dmeg med par bya ba dang/
8b8(rgyud): nor dgrog pa dang/bsdu ba dang/dgugs pa dang/bcing ba dang//bdag nyid gdung ba dang/gsong du gzhug pa dang//long bar bya ba dang//sa bon brlag par bya ba dang//rims dang/dug dang
9a1(rgyud): @#//dbyig dug gis bzung ba dang//sa g.yo ba dang//dmyal bar btong ba dang/byol song gi mngal du sbo ba dang/skyes pa bud med du bya (ba) dang/mngal na gnas pa dbyung ba dang/
9a2(rgyud): chu bsgag pa dang//ma ning du bya ba dang/'dod pa'i khams pa bskyed pa dang/gsad pa dang//gzhan gyis gsod pa du bzhug pa dang/mtshon bcing ba dang//byas chud bsan pa dang/
9a3(rgyud): gcer bu byas pa dang//b+ha ga dang gshang ba'i lcags bsgrog dang/b+ha ga dang bshang pa'i smra ba dang/rgod du gzhug pa dang/khrag dang khu ba dbab pa'i nang gzhom pa dang//khrag

9a5(rgyud): thams cad chags pa dang/dgug pa dang/bskyod/gzhug pa dang//bskyod pa dang//gar dang glu dang//rgod du gzhugs pa dang//mang du smrar bzhug pa dang//thob pa dang dbang du 
9a6(rgyud): bya ba'i las thams cad la sbyar ba dang/bkol ba dang//lha dang/klu dang/gnod sbyin dang/srin po dang/log 'dren dang 'byung bo dang/ma mo dang/kha 'gro ma la sogs pa thams cad

9b2(rgyud): 'di nyid ni//ma byung 'byung bar mi 'gyur zhes//sgyu ma kun dang nyam sbyor ba'i//mkha' 'gro ma zhes rab bgrags pa//rdo rje sems (dpal)de bzhin gshegs//bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs/
9b3(rgyud): da rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro las/las thams cad kyi cho ga sgyu ma chen pos rnam par 'phrul pa'i sngags cho ga rims par phye ba ste//gnyis pa'o////

9b6(rgyud): ang d+ha ma se hi/bo hi ma sau hi//ba hi bu dza hi e so nid d+ho//ma hA nid d+ho/kun ba kan na sas ni na do//sa g+ha ra sa sad d+ha pa da be si ni//sa sa ni da ma b+han hi mi//
9b7(rgyud): ku ku ra sa bi Da la sa tun da ban d+ha mi//dza wa su (ra)ud di di/dza ba ra di bya b+ha pa a di//tad to ba (ma)ha na//a nu ba ta ni//sa du na mo rtsa ri ye//sa tha bdi d+ha na swA hA/zhes
9b8(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs/bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs//sngags 'di lan bdun mngon par bzlas te/khang thog du bzhag na//khyim mi 
10a1(rgyud): @/rnams dran pa med par 'gyuro//pa sa ha to la'i//ma ti ya sa sa dza na mo ha'i//e sa dzi bi dzA tsa hA yi na man ta'i//in de na ha da pa yA dza
10a2(rgyud): me na d+hi na nid da/tam bi shi ma ha sa pa ne na//bde de sa ra dza ka la khan ('ang nye ba du)de dan di//dza tha'i se ba'i ba d+ha tud ti na man ta na//na mo sadi d+he na//na mo bu pa a dz+dza pa ta na/
10a3(rgyud): ba glang gi gdong zhes bya'o//na ma sab rA tUM//tad+ya tha da Di ma hA da ta Di Da du swA hA//sngags bye ma la lan bdun mngon par sngags te zhing la gtor na/ri dgas

10b4(rgyud): t+te b+ha ba g+ha te ba na te/ta bi ku re na sha ta dza//tam ke ha tha ka ral pa ke ga ni ta ma ru ni bi b+ha ta ya//ru dza be ta ra ma ta gu ke to//li ta lu ya//lu nga hi Sh+Ta le ka si ra le

10b8(rgyud): gyis kyang//rlung rnams skrag cing brgyal bar 'gyur//sngags kyi rgyal po 'di'i sbyor ba ni//'di yin te//sngags pa bdag nyid rlung me'i dkyil 'khor la gnas pa'i
11a1(rgyud): @#/ O /rdo rje hUM mdzad//zhal bzhi pa phyag bzhi pa//'dang skyes sngon po lta bu'i mdog can//sngas rgyas lnga'i me tog gis phrang bas dbu brgyan pa/

11a3(rgyud): de nas kham phog gnyis gzung ste/khaM phor gnyis po de la bab blas rlung dang me'i dkyil 'khor rang gi mtshan mas mtshan cing 'bar ba la//klu brgyad kyi dkyil 'khor re'u   
11a4(rgyud): mig dgu par bris te//dkyil 'khor gyi dbusu klu lhag ma can//mgo la gdengs ka bdun gyis brgyan pa//cung zad bskum pa'i phred dkrud can du bri//de'i bla g.yon 
11a5(rgyud): ba la gnas par klu mo gdongs ka gsum dang ldan pas/zhal bzhi 'khyung par bri//de ltar phyi rol gyi dkyil 'khor rnamasu//mtha' yas lasogs pa bris la//

11a7(rgyud): rkang pa rnams kyang klu gzhan gyis bcings ste//de dag gi gdong ka la yang hUM phu hUM bri'o//de nas oM hri a ni la tar be ga pri ti min da ra she ya hi phu sarba na ga ku la na na 
11a8(rgyud): ya phu//sa ma ya pra bre sha ya phu//sarba na sa da ya phu phu phu//zhes bye ba 'dis dkyil 'khor bskor la//yang sngags 'di dang phyag rgyas so so'i lus la bkug ste/
11b1(rgyud): gzhug par bya'o//phyag rgya ni 'di yin te//mthe bo kha sbyar te bcings la//phyi rol tu bzhag ste//sor mo lhag ma rnams gdengs bka' ltar bya'o//dza hUM baM ho zhes bya ba sngags 
11b2(rgyud): mthar bzlas zhing//dgug pa dang gzhug pa la sogs pa byas la//de nas kham pher der mar nag tsha bas bkang ste//sdod bu la (ma)spar la kham phor gzhan gyis bkang ste//
11b3(rgyud): kham phor de ri rab tu bsgoms nas//de'i steng du hUM gis khro bo'i dkyil 'khor bsgoms la//rdo rje hUM mdzad kyi sngags (bu)bas brgya rtsa brgyad zlas te//chog bzhin du     
11b4(rgyud): bzhag pa bya'o/de nas kham phor gyi steng du bsgoms pa'i rdo rje hUM mdzad la sogs pa'i dkyil (pa)'khor ba'i lus kyi gzar (bu)las byung ba'i chu//rdo rje nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor la mthor 
11b5(rgyud): bar bsam la/bdag rdo rje hUM mdzad kyi sbyor ba dang ldan pas//zhal bzhi nas hUM bzhi sgrogs shing//rdo rje hUM mdzad (phyag rgya)kyi bcings pa'i 'dzub mo'i rtse mor hUM gnyis bsgoms te//
11b6(rgyud): de las byung ba'i me lce'i phreng bas sprin rnams zlog par bsams te//dpal rdo rje hUM mdzad kyi sngag kyang rlung bzla'o//sbyor ba 'dis ni dkyil 'khor gyi phyi rol khru brgyad kyi 
11b7(rgyud): bar chad bzlog par bya'o//dkyil 'khor chen po'i sbyor ba yis//phyi yi dkyil 'khor bri bar bya//kha dog dkar po mdzes pa la//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dbus gan(sa)
11b8(rgyud): pa'i|ye shes sa bon bsgoms par bya//rlung dang me yi dbus gnas par//chu('i) dkyil 'khor bsgom par bya//dbang chen dbu(dkyil) 'khor dbus su ni//
12a1(rgyud): @#/ O /gsal ba'i dkyil 'khor rab bsgoms te//rdo rje mkha'agro bsgoms bya ba//zhal cig ni phyag bzhi pa ste//glang chen sten la g.yas rkyang 
12a2(rgyud): bzhugs//spyan bsum skra ni grol bar brgyan//gzugs bzang bzhon pa phun sum tshogs//thod pa'i phreng bas rnam par grubs/kha dog dmar po gsal ba'i 
12a3(rgyud): mdang //shin du mdzes pa'i nam bza' gsol//'bar ba'i ral gri thod pa dang//mngar dang gzhu ni 'gengs ba ste/khrag la bges pa hre las byung//'od zer dmar 
12a4(rgyud): bo'i phreng 'khrugs shing//'jigs pa dag kyang 'jigs par mdzad//gru bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa'i//mkha'agro ma'i 'khor lo bsgom//bla re dam pa bris pa dang/

14a1(rgyud): @#/ O /las gyur pa bsams la/chu'i steng du yang pad+ma 'dab brgyad bris na/lte ba la hUM gnyis kyis bzir ba'i nang bri/'dab ma brgyad la yang klu brgyad kyi snying po gzir bar 
14a2(rgyud): bris la/stobs po che'i sngags dang /hUM dgus cho ga bzhin du mtsho skyes bskor te /sngags bzlas (ni )ji ltar byung ba bzhin mchod pa byas la/kham phor kha sbyar tu klu'i gnasu bcug na/
14a3(rgyud): klu thams cad char gyi rgyun 'bebaso//gal te des sdang pa'i klu rnams char mi 'bebs na/der sbyor ba 'di ltar bya ste/gtum po'i sa bon la lan rtsa brgyad bzlas la klu'i mtshor/sbyin 
14a4(rgyud): sreg byas/na 'char 'ab bar 'gyuro//gal te des kyang char 'bab par ma gyur na/de'i tshe yung dkar dang /tsha ba'i mar dang /sbrang rtsi dang /ka ra bi ri'i me tog rnams/lhan cig phye mar

14a6(rgyud): 'gyur ro//gal te yang mi 'bab na/dur khrod kyi thal ba bzung ste/rtsabs skyur por spangs nas sngags bzlas te mtshor bcug na/klu rnams myos shing char gyi rgyun 'bebaso//
14a7(rgyud): ji ste des kyang ma 'babs na/pad+ma (ma)dang ud dpal sngon me dang /bab bla zangs kyi phye ma dang /yungs kar dang /dbang po lag pa dang /pa la sha rnams las ril bu rgya shug gi 
14a8(rgyud): 'bru bu tsam byas la /ri lu re re zhing yang lan bdun bdun sngon par sngags te/ji srid char mi 'bab kyi bar du/ril lu rnams mtshor bskyur gyi bar du nyin mo dang mtshan mo bye 
14b1(rgyud): brag med pa'i char 'babs par 'gyur ro//de nas bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin chen pos/sngags kyi rgyal po 'di gsungs te////oM na mo nI la aM ba d+ha ra ya badzrad na
14b2(rgyud): ye/mahA yak+Sha se na pa ta ye/a ne ga ga gaM ga na di pa lu ga/u pa ma bud d+hA kro Ti sha Ta sa ha sra pa ri bring ta ye bi kri ta taM sh+Ta ka ra ka la ru pa ya/ag ni ra si na ya/dza li ta 
14b3(rgyud): bi sha swa sa ya b+hi ma na da ya/ba su ki na ga ra dza b+hi bu shi ta ka ra tsa ra Na ya kri k+Sha b+huM dza ga ya/dzo b+ha bi ta ya dz+wa li/b+hu dzaM ga ma ni ma ku ta ya yu ri ta to pa ba da na ya saM/ku tsi ta 
14b4(rgyud): b+hu la la Ta ya/ta sha na a ba sh+Ta ro s+tha ya krid ta    ru p+pa/bi dud dzi hA ya ma ho ra gi bag k+Sha na ya ka ra/po ta tra sa na ka ra ya/su ba ra na na ga rA dza me kha la ya/hUM ka ra nad 
14b5(rgyud): kra sa na ka ra ya a si ti dzo tsa na sa ha prag bi ra sra ga ra dza nu mar du la ya/tsan da a di da skan da ba ha ra ya/ngaM shA ka ra la ya/ha si ta ta ha sa na da na da ya/mahA saM k+sho ba ka ra 
14b6(rgyud): ya/ka la ru pa ya tre lo ka bag sha k+sha na ka ra ya/na ga ni ma tu ga la/i mang ga ga na ti ba li ga sa me b+hud d+he pa ga bad a bir ba sha taM man Ta la ar tra yi sha mi/bo bo ban na gaa 
14b7(rgyud): ti pa ta ye/dan nI la aM ba ra sha ri ram/badzra pa ne/hri da yi/ma ti pra ma tha/ma ku lod sa da naMb b+bi bya ti/tad+ya thA/bud d+ha bud d+ha/bi bud d+ha bi bud d+ha/mahA pa la lo ga pa li ne dza/
14b8(rgyud): ha ra ha ra na ga naM/pra ha ra pra ha ra/sarba na ga naM/tshin d+ha tshin d+ha na ga naM/hri da ya ni/b+hin d+ha byin d+ha na ga mar ni/bi shir yan du ga hri da yi ni spo da ya spo da ya/ga na ya/na ni bi shir

20b6(rgyud): bcos 'dra//ku sum dza yi me tog dang //me 'bar sol ba spungs 'dra zhing //nyi ma 'char dka'i 'od 'dra ba'i sbyan zur bkrad pas 'jigs par mdzad//shin du drag pa'i 'jugs

20b8(rgyud): ru/yangs pa'i rdo rje mda' yis ni//dra ba chen pos 'khor lo bcings//khab gdong la sogs so so yi//ba sbu re re'i khung nas kyang //ri rab du mi' rdul mtshungs pa'i/mkha' 
22a1(rgyud): @#/ O /'gro ma'i 'khor lo dang //rdo rje gsum gyi rang bzhan rnams//spro bas (na)ma mkha' gsal mdzad pa//phyag brgyad spyan gsum gyen ska 'bar//mkha' 
22a2(rgyud): 'gro nyi shu bzhi phyed bskor//de ltar sngags pas rnam pasam bya//g.yas dang po na rdo rje sngon po 'bar zhing rno ba gdengs pa la//rigs ldan gyis bkris pa bsnams pa'o/gnyis pa 
22a3(rgyud): na pad mas bcings pa'i mda'i mchog go/gsum pa na pad+ma chen pos yu ba bkris pa'i rwa la gri'o//bzhi pa na dung skyong gi zhags pas dkris pa'i lcags kyu'o//g.yon gyi dang po na mtha' 
22a4(rgyud): yas kyi ba dan btags pa'i kha T+waM bsgreng ba'o//gnyis pa na nor rgyas kyi bu y+si rgyud byas pa'i gzhu'i mchog go/gsum pa na 'jog po zhags pa dang sdig 'dzub po//bzhi pa na tshangs pa'i

22a7(rgyud): pa bsnams ba/thod pa'i phreng bas klubs shing /gcer bu zhal ba gartsigs pa/'od zer sna tshogs 'phro ba/khro gnyer gyi rim pa can/zur mig du gzigs pas sa bzhi bskyod pa༐

22b2(rgyud): khri dang sres la spang rtsis sbrus te mgo bdun pa'i klu'i gzugs sor bcu gnyis pa byas la//de'i khang pa sor bcu drug yangs par byas nas khang pa de ba (I) (I)zhil gdong bzhi byas te/gru gcig

22b7(rgyud): yin na yang /rlung grang me dang rde char dang klog dang ser ba 'bebs te de bzlog par 'dod na/rig pa 'dzin pa'i yungs kar dang thal bzho gnyis tsam la/lan nyi shu'i bar du bzlas te//
22b8(rgyud): ji srid 'dod pa'i sa phyogs su 'tshams bcings la/yang de nyid la na bdun bzlas te klu'i ma gor bsnun zhing /yid kyis ji snyed 'dod par phyags bcing bar bya ste/phyogs 'tsham 
23a1(rgyud): rdo rje mkhro' @#/ O /dang steng 'og gtor na/'tshams chen po bcing bar 'gyur ro//ji ste dgra thams cad dang 'gran dang zla dang sdang ba'i grogs po dang /sdang ba'i phyogs byed

23a3(rgyud): mo de nyid dud khrod kyi thal ba dang bsgos te/skyes pa 'am bud med kyi gzugs byas la/khro bo chen po'i sngags brgya rtsa brgyad zlas te/dur khrod kyi sa brkos pa'i nad du ming nas

23b1(rgyud): gsol to//sgra chen pos rnam par 'phrul pa'i klu'i sngags mi 'tshal na/dka' drin chen pos gshadu gsol/bka' stsal pa/sngags kyi rgyal po chen po'i mtshan nyid skal pa/

23b7(rgyud): zla ba'i dum bus brgyan pa ste//slar yang dbu' ma'i dbu'i ma bzung //byang phyogs kyi ni dbu ma yi/zhabs kyi pad+ma nyi mar ldan/bdan bral ba ni sme bas brgyan//'bar ba 'bugs pas   
23b8(rgyud): rnam par klubs/byang phyogs kyi ni dang po dang /khyu mchog steng na gnas pa yi/gshin rje phyogs kyi mthar gnas dang //rung ni yu sa mo'i brgyan/bden bral la ni gnas pa yi//
24a1(rgyud): @#/ O /gshin rje'i ni mche ba can/gti mug brgyan gyi rnam par brgyan//gshin rje 'joms ma gti mug klubs//klu'i phyog rgya 'di yis ni/mkha'agro ma kun dga' 
24a2(rgyud): 'gur 'gyur//sgyu 'phrul chen po bcu drug 'di//yi ge re re kun gnas zhes/mkha 'gre ma kun mnyam sbyor ba'i/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis 
24a3(rgyud): gsungs/dang po rus spal gzung bar bya//bzang po'i steng du gnas pa yi//byang gi dang po gnyis par ldan/gshin rje pho nya chu ltar bzhugs//yi ge ra ni gti mug brgyan//

24b1(rgyud): bskrad par 'gyur/oM in de na ha da ba ya/dza me na u pa sa ya rdzi a/hri d+ha pram dar te na/tang dz+dzi ya su la ha re na su li yA swA hA/oM dzaM b+hi ni dza b+he hi/oM zaM ba ni thaM b+ha hi/oM mo

24b7(rgyud): la/ji srid mi snang bar gyur pa de srid du bzlas par bya'o//sprul gyi rgyal po rnyed pa dang /ri mo dkar nag dum bu saur phye la/dkar nag so sor shes par byas pa de dang /shang

25a1(rgyud): @#/ O /snam du byas te/zla ba yar ngo la dum bu dkar pos bdug ge/mar ngo l+sa ni dum bu nag pos bdug go/de nas de gyon pa'i lus kyis lan khri bzlas na 'grub /

27b1(rgyud): po taM gis ni phyag 'tshal ba'o//pra ti pe taM gi ni phyag gi lan no//ga mum mi 'gra ba'o//lum ba la ni (rang)zhes pa'o//ni ga ram mi byin cig pa'o//tsa bu kaM ni long shig pa'o//hrid 
27b2(rgyud): ya ni dpa'o//kur ba ni shi ba'o//kar ni kar ni dril bu'o//a li ka ra ni mgo be'o//ba ra hi ni skra'o//shra ba no ni rna ba'o//man tha ni bdud rtsi'o//na ra ni mnyam par

27b8(rgyud): mahA k+sha ra ni phyugs chen po'o//che ni ra'o//na ni skyes bu'o//ba ni glang do//ma ni ma he'o//ba ni bza' ba'o//a s+ya ka ni rgyal po'i skyes bu'o//s+tha ni gnas pa'o//kha la 
28a1(rgyud): @#/ O /reg pa ni zos pa'o//so la reg pa ni ngoms pa'o//hre ka ni ngo tsha ba'o//steng pa la reg pa ni me thun gyis shig pa'o//brla la reg pa ni de ltar gyis    
28a2(rgyud): shig pa'o//zhes mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba'i//rdo rje mkha'agro bde ba'i mchog/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs//rgyud   
28a3(rgyud): kyi rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro las//mkho 'gro ma thamad kyi brda'i rim par phye ba ste//brgyad pa'o////de lha mo thams cad yang dag par 'dus te//
28a4(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das thog ma dang tha ma med pa'i rnal 'byor gyi dbang phyug chen po la gsol ba btab pas//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thamad kyi gsang chen//snang ba    
28a5(rgyud): thams cad 'dul ba'i 'khrul 'khor gyi le'u zhes bya ba bskyed du gsol//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis//de bzhin gshegs pa tha mad kyi gsang chen mchog du rmad du 'gyur pa//
28a6(rgyud): ring chen kha sbyor zhes bya ba'i 'khrul 'khor gsungs pa ni//mdor sdus nas ni brjod par bya//de ni bdag gi bshad gyi nyon//rig ldan dang ni rta'i 'gros//gshin rje yi dgas de bzhin
28a7(rgyud): du|gzhan yang ro langs zung dang ni//srin po yi ni khyad par gyi//de nas rim dang phyi rol rim//des 'jom sgo dang stong pa dang //gwa'i mthar ni rdo rje ldan//'khrul 'khor 
28a8(rgyud): chen po bcu gnyis dang //sngags gyi phreng bas khyad par byas//rim pa 'di bzhin brjod pa yin//gru bzhi shin tu tshad ldan yin//re mig bzhi bcu dgur ldan pa//sngags pas 
28b1(rgyud): mnyam par bzhag ste bya//dang po yi ni phreng ba gnyis//lcag bcas a las sogs pa dged//re mig lhag ma rnams la ni//ka las sogs pa mdor bsdus pa'i//yige    
28b2(rgyud): thams cad ci rigs par//go rims bzhin du dgod par bya//dbus kyis re mig stong pa la//ka sha zhes bya ba'i yi ge dgod//de nas pa ni bri bar bya//'khor (rje)lo ri mo gnyis   
28b3(rgyud): bskor la//ri mo'i bar du rnal 'byor pas//'phel bas logs bsdus te bgod//phyi rol pad ma 'dapa   brgyad la//dngang por ka ste lus ngan tsa//chu lha gshin (rje) dngata//rlung dang 
28b4(rgyud): me dang dbang chen chur//de ltar ya ra la wa dgod//'byung ba chen po khams zhes bya//slar yang phyi rol gru bzhi la//rlung dang me dang bdud rtsi sog//gshin rje 'joms ma la sogs
28b5(rgyud): ste/sha khA sa har bstan pa dgod//ri rI li lI zhes bya ba//ma ching zhes byar bstan pa (ba)zhi//sgo bzhi ru ni dgod par bya//chog ga'i sbyor ba de lha bus//sngags kyi rgyal mo de nas dgod/

31a1(rgyud): @#/ O /pas re'u mig sum bcu brgyan//dbu ma'i dbu ma bzun nas ni//gti mug gis ni mdzes byas pa//sum bcu gcig par gnas par bya//ma ga ra zhon rmi lam dang//

33b5(rgyud): rlung gi phyi rol bzung nasani//rusbal rmi lam zla ba can//sum bcu gcig la nyis 'gyur bri//slar yang rlung gi phyi rol bri//rusbal rmi lam zla ba can //sum bcu gsum par

33b7(rgyud): ba 'ang sbyin byas ste//lha'i mtha' ni bzung nas su//slar yangade bzhin rlung dang ni//rlung gi phyi rol bzung nas ni//'bugs pa yi ni brgyan ba dag/bzhi bcu gsum pa     
33b8(rgyud): bri bar bya//dbu ma'i gnyis pa bzung nas ni//nub kyi dang po brgyan pa yis//bzhi bcu lnga pa mdzes par bya//ri zhes bya ba'i stan gnas pa'i //dang po yi ni gsum  
34a1(rgyud): @#/ O /pa dang //lho mtha' stan la gnas pa yi//rlung gi phyi rol bzung ba la//gti mug gis ni mdzes par bya//dbu ma'i dang po bzung nas su//
34a2(rgyud): rlung yang der ni sbyin pa yis//bzhi bcu bdun pa brgyan par bya//'bugs pa rmi lam zla ba can//shar gyi gsum pa bzung nas su//ri zhes bya ba sbyin par bya//lha mtha' 
34a3(rgyud): stan la gnas pa yis//rlung gi phyi rol de nas bzung //gti mug gis ni mdze par bya//dbu ma'i dang pi bzung nas su//de bzhin rlung dang bcas pa yis//re'u mig    
34a4(rgyud): bzhi bcu dgu pa la//rgyan pa mchog tu phan pa yin//'khor lo 'di ni lha mo che//mchog tu rno ste brlag par byed//mar ngo yi ni tshis bcu dang //de bzhin yar ngo de nyid     
34a5(rgyud): dam //yang na 'byung po'i mtshan mo la//thod pa'i stan la gnas nas su//lho phyogs su ni kha ltas nas//dug dang khrag dang gtum pa yi//sa bon gyis ni snag tsha dang //
34a6(rgyud): bar snang la ni thod pa blugs//mi yi rus pa'i smyug gu yis//'jigs byed 'jigs pa'i sngags rnams btu //dang po sha chen gtor ma sbyin//de nas las ni rtsam     
34a7(rgyud): par bya//dmar po 'am ni sngon po yi//gos ni khyad par dag tu bgo/mchod pa rdo rje go cha dang //de bzhin bsam gtan zlas pa dag/ji ltar sus kyang mi rtogs pa//  
34a8(rgyud): de ltar sngags pas rtag tu bya//bsad la gshin rje'i rgyal po ste//yi dgas la ni yi dgas gzugs//ro langs la ni ro langs dang //srin po srin pos gnan   
34b1(rgyud): par bya//stong la stong pa'i pad ma ste//nges par 'joms pa'i grwa ru ni//sngags phreng de bzhin phreng ba yi//rim pa de ltar brjod pa yin//'di ni khams gsum rgyal 
34b2(rgyud): zhes bya'i//sngags tshig bzhi yis rnam brgyan pa//rigs rgyal 'khor lo bsgyur ba'i sngags//ma byung 'byung bar mi 'gyur ro//'khrul 'khor chen po bcu gnyis  
34b3(rgyud): ni//bzhin mdzes ma ni khyod la bshad //'di ni sdang ba thaMs cad kyi//de bzhin 'jigs byed mi bzad yin//yang dag cho ga gang gis ni//dngos grub myur du  
34b4(rgyud): 'jigs byed pa//bza' dang ston mo 'dus pa yi//bsgrub thabs cho ga de nas bshad//phyugs rnams dag ni dgug pa nyid//sgrub pos sngar stan rim pas bya//  
34b5(rgyud): rgyu med pa'i phyugs rnams ni//rgyu mtshan med pas bzhom par bya//phyugs rnams dang ni de bzhin lnga//bong bu mi dang rus sbal dang //rnga mo lce sbyang rta la sogs//khyi 
34b6(rgyud): dang phag gi sha dag dang //zangs kyi gtsug phud can de bzhin//bza' dang sbyin bsreg gtor ma ni//ma dpyad par ni byas gyur na//ma lus la med 'grub cing //

35a1(rgyud): @#/ O /dgu pa'o////tshe dang nad med spel ba dang //bya dang bya min sbyor ba'i mchog /dbugs kyi las la sogs mtshan nyid//nyon cig/

35b6(rgyud): pa ni gti mug go/drug pa ni bzang po'o//bdun pa ni khyu mchog go/brgyad pa ni rusbal lo//dgu pa ni chu srin no//bcu pa ni yugs sa mo'o/bcu gcig pa ni

36b7(rgyud): dgu 'aM//lo gu na shi chad du ma gyur na//phyug cing bskal pa bzang ba dang //sems gdug cing brtsu ba la//me drod chung zhing yul mtha' 'khob kyi gtso bor gyur la//   
36b8(rgyud): g.yem la dga' ba dang //thams cad 'jam por smra zhing //nor dang ldan zhing nad med pa dang //skyid la rtag par 'gyur te//tshe lo brgya rtsa gcig 'tsho ste//sa'i   
37a1(rgyud): @#/ O /dbang phyug gi nyi ma la 'chi bar 'gyur ro//sa'i dbang phyug gi nyi ma la btsas pa'i byis pa ni//nyi ma brgyad pa la 'i//lo brgyad pa la gal te shi 
37a2(rgyud): chad du ma gyur na/nor dang dang 'bru dang rta dang ldan par 'gyur zhing bden par smra la/mdza shes mang zhing chos shes pa dang /mkhas la tshangs par spyod cing //sems can kun la phan 
37a3(rgyud): byed la/bzo shes pa dang /'jam por smra zhing skal pa dang ldan te//stan chos thams cad rgyas par 'gyur ba la the tshom med de/tshe lo brgya bcu rtsa brgyad 'tsho ste/rmi lam gyi nyi ma la 
37a4(rgyud): 'chi bar 'gyur ro//rmi lam gyi nyi ma la btsas pa'i byis pa ni/nyi ma bzhi 'am lnga 'am bcu 'am/min na zla ba brgyad dam/lo brgyad pa la gal te shi chad du ma gyur na/des bla ma

37a8(rgyud): byis pa ni /zla ba bzhi pa dang drug pa dang dgu pa dang /bcu pa la gal te shi chad du ma gyur na/de ni shes rab chung zhing blo dang me drod dman la/sdang ba mang zhing bu tsha mi 'tsho la    
37b1(rgyud): 'khor mang ba dang /yo byad 'phel zhing sdug bsngal mang ba dang /gnyen bdun thams cad dga' ba/ngan song la rtse ba dang /nor dang /phyugs mang ba /chung ma gsum len cing /

37b6(rgyud): dgu pa 'am/lo dgu pa la gal te shi chad du gyur na/des ni rang gi rigs bstod bcing chos shes pa dang /bstan chos phun sum 'tshogs shing bzo sna tshogs rig pa 
37b7(rgyud): dang /dpa' la nga rgyal che ba /g.yo sgyu can yin la/phra dog dang ldan zhing nor nyung ba dang /gzugs bzang zhing bya ba thams cad yang ba yin te/tshe lo drug bcu rtsa gsuM
37b8(rgyud): bde bar 'tsho la/yugs mo yi nyi ma la chur 'chi ba gdon mi za'o//yug sa mo'i nyi ma la btsas pa'i byis pa ni/nyi ma gnyis pa 'am bcu pa 'am/zla ba gnyis pa 'i 
38a1(rgyud): rdo mkhra'o @#/ O //rgyad pa 'am/lo brgyad pa la gal te shi chad dum gyur na/de ni sdig pa'i blo dang ldan zhing /sems can kun la 'khyal tshig smra zhing nor nyung la 'phyar ba /

39a1(rgyud): rdo+e mkhro' @#/ O /can du 'gyur ba dang /ngang tshul ngan zhing sdig pa spyod la/tshig rtsub dang 'khyal pa smra la dga' ba dang /mtshon cha rdeg pa la dga' ste/tshe lo brgya

39b4(rgyud): yis bshad kyis nyon//sa yi cha ni yid 'ong la//kha dog dkar po dri yis byug/me tog phreng dkar gyis brgyan cing //rgyan mtshan zla res 'bar byas par rtsa bar nag po
39b5(rgyud): ra ri med//bum pa chu yis dkang sogs pa//de dbus lha mo bsam pa ni//pad+ma'i btan la yang dag bzhugs//zhal cig phyag ni gnyis pa ste//shin du mdzes 
39b6(rgyud): pa'i na bzas klubs//rgyan rnams kun gyis brgyan pa ste//pad+ma'i phreng bas mdzes par byas//mkha' 'gro ma'i 'khor lo spro//gangs dang kun d+ha zla 'dra pa'i//
39b7(rgyud): bdud rtsi'i chu rgyun 'bab pa yis//dug gi mthu na phrog par mdzad//lha mo'i byin pa 'di lta bu//bsams na dug ni 'joms par 'gyur//'o na nag por gyur gyi
39b8(rgyud): bar//mun pas ring du zil gnon pa//slar yang 'dug pas bdug par bya//dbyid zla las sogs rim bzhin du//nyi ma byang du 'gro ba'i tshe//ji ltar dus bzhin   
40a1(rgyud): @//mtshan ma ni//rim gyis rim gyis sbyar bar bya//nyin mo'i yi ni chu tshod dag/mkhas bas bzhi dang bcu gnyis bsgyur//mtshan mo drug dang bcu gsum

46b1(rgyud): pa'i dkon mchog gsum//deng nas brtsams te mchog du bzung//slab pa yi ni tshul khrims dang //dge ba'i chos ni bsdud pa dang//sems can don byed tshul khrims gsum
46b2(rgyud): bdag gis so sor btan por bzung//rdo rje rigs mtha' chen po la//rdo rje dril bu phyag rgya yang //yang dag nyid du bzung bgyi zhing //slob dpon dag kyang bdag gis bzung //rin chen
46b3(rgyud): rigs chen rnal 'byor bgyi//dam tshig yid du 'ong ba la//nyin re bzhin ni dus drug du//sbyin pa rnam bzhi brtsal bar bgyi//byang chub chen po las 'byung ba'i//pad+ma'i rigs 
46b4(rgyud): chen dag pa la//phyi nang gsang ba theg pa gsum//dam chos yang dag bzung bar bgyi//las kyi rigs chog chen po la//mchod pa ci nus bgyid pa dang//sdom pa thams cad 
46b5(rgyud): ldan par bgyi//bla na med pa'i byang chub sems//dam pe de ni skyed bgyi zhing//sems can kun gyi don gyi phyir//sdom pa ma lus bzung bar bgyi//ma rgyal ba rnams dgral bdag/
46b6(rgyud): ma grol ba rnams bdag gis dgrol//dbugs ma phyin pa dbugs dbyung zhing //'gro kun mya ngan 'das la dgod//de nas oM swa b+ha ba shud d+ha sa rba d+ha rma swa b+ha wa shud d+ho haM//

46b8(rgyud): chen po//thong pa'i phreng ba'i rnam par klubs//zhal bzhi pa la phyag bzhi pa//spyan gsum gyi ni mdzes pa ste//yi dgas chen po gdan du bya//nyi ma la bzhugs gar 
47a1(rgyud): @#/ O /dang ldan//phyag na thod pa khwa T+wAM dang//de bzhin gzhan yang gzhu dang mda'//mchog gi phyag rgya de nyid kyi//ye shes rgya mtsho bsgom par bya//shar ni dkar 
47a2(rgyud): zhing sngo ste//g.yon na khwa T+wAM dril bu dang//gzhan na rdo rje thod pa'o//byang phyogs mar dag(sa) sor skya'i 'od//g.yon pa khwa T+wAM zhags pa dang//g.yas na rnga chung thod pa snams//nub 
47a3(rgyud): phyogs dmar zhing ser skya'i 'od//gzhu dang mda'i mchog dang ni//sna tshogs pad+ma thed pa dag//g.yon dang g.yas kyi phyag du dgod//lho ni dkar zhing zhi ba'i 'od//g.yon na dud    
47a4(rgyud): dang rin chen dang//g.yas na ba ngan thod pa bsnams//glang chen lce spyang mi dang ni//glang gi thod par rim bzhin bzhugs//dang po ltung byed ma yin te//gnyis pa de bzhin bsod ma yin/

47a7(rgyud): ldan pa ru//bsgoms na skad cig de la 'bar//rdzas kyi dngos grub 'dras rab ster//lha me de ltar bsgoms nas ni//sbyor ba'i dngos grus rig pa che//yid ches

47b3(rgyud): bsgoms bar bya//gsang chen dam pa de dag ni//mdzes ma khyed la bshad pa yin//tho rangs rang gi snyir khar ni//a las zla ba'i dkyil 'khor bsam//de steng yi ge

47b8(rgyud): nang logs su//dkyil 'khor lnga ni rnam brgyan par//ri rab lhun po'i steng du ni//sna tshogs pad+ma yid 'ong ma//mkha'agro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba'i//rdo rje mkha'

48b7(rgyud): sems dpa' ste//gnyis pa pad ma gar dbang rgyal//gsum pa rnam par snang mdzad de//de bzhin bzhi pa rta mchog rgyal//lnga pa rdo rje nyi ma ste//drug pa he ru kar ni brjod//

49a1(rgyud): @#/ O /pa drug//de nas dbang skur bsgoms nas ni//phyi nas mchod pa tsam par bya//bdag nyid he ru kar byas nas//dpal ldan he ru ka bran bya//dam tshig ye

49b1(rgyud): gsungasu//mig ni de bi ko Ti te//dpung pa ma la ba zhes brjod//'di dag nye ba'i nas yin te//gnas kyi khyad par tshul gyis ni//mkha' sbyong thugs kyi 'khor lor

52b5(rgyud): dpa' de bzhin gshegs//bcom ldan rdo rje can gyi gsung //rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje mkha' gro las//sku gsung dang sngags dang rgyud kyi 'byung ba dang //'chi ba bslu ba zhes 
52b6(rgyud): bya ba'i rim pa phye ba ste////bcwo lnga pa'o////de nas dngos grub kun gyi gnas//gsang ba mchog gi dges pa ste//yid 'ong bcud ni kun gyi rten//sems 
52b7(rgyud): can kun la bde ster ba//'dod pa'i yon tan lnga yis rgyan//bla re dam pa (yi dbye ba)bres pas 'bar//'dab stong ldan pa'i pad ma che//sbyor ba'i gdan la bzhugs pa la//der ni lha mo sa 
52b8(rgyud): zhus gyur pa//'khor lo kun gyi des pa dang /dbyangs rnams kyi ni bye (gtang )ba mi mtshal te//'khor lo'i grangs ni ji lta bu//de rnams las ni ci zhig lags//sna tshogs glu yi sngags dang ni/
53a1(rgyud): @/ O /'khrul 'khor dag ni mdor bsdus dang /'khor lo'i dbang phyug dus dang ni//de bzhin dpa' bo rnams kyi stan//'di dag la the tshom mchis//bde ba chen;
53a2(rgyud): po bshad du gsol//bka' lcal pa//rgyud rnams kun tu sbas pa gang//de dag thams cad zhu 'dod pa//legaso logaso lha mo che//blo gros chen mo legaso legs//
53a3(rgyud): mkha'agro min thams cad kyang//the tshom skye na gzhan ci smros//sgyu ma kun tu rnam 'phrul ba//nyon cig lha mo sgyu ma che//gang zhig bris pa tsam gyis ni//sgrub pos dngos grub 
53a4(rgyud): thob gyur ba//mdor sdus nas ni brjod bya ste//de ni bdag gis bshad gyis nyon//gachag dang dmar rab rtag gyur nas//khyad par du ni gtor ma sbyin//tsa ru'i bza' ba lhan cig 
53a5(rgyud): bya/'di yis sgrub po kun la ni//ma mo dga' 'gyur the tshom med//de nas mkha'agro ma rnams ni//sa rnams la ni rnam rol ba//brtson chen 'khor lo bsgyur ma dang//stob+sa 
53a6(rgyud): chen ma deng rab dpa' mo//'khor lo'i go cha glang rnam(dang)//kha Da ro ha 'khor lo'i shugs//bya gdong ma dang rta na mo//shin du bzang mo sngo bsangs mo//chang 'thung ma dang rlung 
53a7(rgyud): shugasaM//de bzhin 'jigs byed chen mo dang//sa bsrungs ma dang ljon grib ma//lang ka'i bdag mo mi'u thung mo// (dpa' ba'i slo ma sna chen mo)//'od ldan ma dang gtum mig mi//rab gtum ma ste bsgrub 
53a8(rgyud): byed yin//mkha'agro ma(na)nyi shu bzhi//'di dag sngon nyid grub pa yin//rang ming brjod pa'i sngags la ni//mthar la ni hUM hUM phaT dang sbyor//oM ni thams cad gsal byed do//
53b1(rgyud): grub gsol 'dod pa thams cad la//rnal 'byor dam chog 'di mthong na//gzhan dag snying po med brtsar mtshungs//dpa' bo nying gur grub pa ni//dpal ldan he ru ka yi ni/
53b2(rgyud): dkyil 'khor dam pa mthong ba yin/de nas de shes rtag bsgom pa//dngos grub de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs//nyon cig lha mo she rab can//rig pa'i rgyal mo bsgrub 
53b3(rgyud): pa'i thabs//'khor lo chen po rmad gyur pa//gdeng ka'i dbang phyug zhes bya ste//thos pa tsam gyis bsreg byed na//ming dang ldan par bris pa yis//gding ka thams cad   
53b4(rgyud): smos ci dgos//dbang ldan nas ni brtsams nas su//klu rnams sa bon las byung ba//rin chen dang ldan gdengs gsum mtho//gzhan yang phyogs ni kun tu bsam//de ma nas
53b5(rgyud): mgur du 'di bzhes te//shar du kwa mang bar du bri//nub du de he ste byang du he//gshin rje hU+e zhes ba bri//ki li ki li'i yi dgasu//srin bu tsi li si li bri//me yi 'tshangs ni 
53b6(rgyud): mtha' dang bcas//dang po rab tum ma nas ni//brtson chen ma'i mthar thug pa//sngags 'di dag ni byin gyis brlab//'khor lo 'di ni skal chen ldan//dgra rnams kyi ni      
53b7(rgyud): gnad gcod pa//hU+e phaT zhes bya spel ba yi//sgrub bya  de'i dbu su bri     lan cig bzlas pa'i gu gul gyis//bdug sa nyid du sbyar byas la//klu rnams thams cad 'beb par 
53b8(rgyud): 'gyur//lag pa g.yon pa la 'khor lo 'di dngos su 'am yid kyis bris la sngags//lan gcig bzlas pas bya bas na//'che ba dug gis zin pa slong ba byed do/
54a1(rgyud): @#/ O /ka ra bi ra'i bsnyug gu bris te//lan cig bzlas (pas)rig na mngal gang du 'pho bar 'dod pa der 'pho bar 'gyur ro//dug dang lan tsha dang ske tshes bris te/
54a2(rgyud): khros na gsod par 'gyur ro//ro bsregs pa'i 'ga' ba da du ra'i khu bas me bsad de//'byung po'i nyi ma la de nyid kyis 'khor lo bris la//lan cig bzlas  
54a3(rgyud): pas de gang la bsnun pa de ltung bar 'gyuro//sang ka ti' gasnyug gus sngags pas cho ga snga ma bzhin bris la//thal ba la mngon par sngags te//gang la snun pa de  
54a4(rgyud): sha zas bzung bar 'gyur ro//dung khrod kyi sol bas shin leb bam rtsig ngos la/'byung po'i mtshan ma gang gi snying khar bris pa de//gdon chen pos bzung bar 'gyuro/
54a5(rgyud): bsgrub bya'i gzugs bcug la//de'i snying khar 'khrul 'khor bcug ste//zhag bdun gyi bar du dri'i chu'i nang du bzhag nas//a ti mug la'i lcug ma la lan bdun   
54a6(rgyud): sngags ste de la bsnun na zhag bdun gyi mthar rlung gi shugs kyis 'od bar 'gyur ro//'khor lo de nyid bye brag du dur khrod kyi Ira sa(sa)la//pho nya'i kha dog gis bye brag du 
54a7(rgyud): gyur pa bris la//las kyi pho nya dang lhan cig du sgrub par bya'o//ka ra bi ra'i lcug ma khru phyed dang gnyis kyi tshang dang ldan pas//gang la mngon par phyogs te sngags 
54a8(rgyud): bris nas//ro bsregs pa'i me 'bar ba la dor te//ji sring ma tshig par myur du blangs la//rig pa'i rgyal po lan bdun bzlas nas//gang zhig dran de klang la    
54b1(rgyud): bskor na//de de'i mod la rlung gi shugs kyis 'ong bar 'gyur ro//skyes pa'i ro'i kham phor la//bab la dang yung ba'i khu bas gang gi ming bris pa de de'i mod 
54b2(rgyud): la bcings par 'gyuro//dur khrod kyi sar sa ras zla mtshan gyi ras la//shing a sho ka'i bsnyug gus//klu rnams spangs pa'i 'khor lo 'di bris la//
54b3(rgyud): de'i dbus su sngags pas cho ga bzhin yige e dang spel ba bris na//mkha'agro ma'i khyad par thams cad dang //lus sru+il po thams bcings par 'gyuro/
54b4(rgyud): thod pa la skyes pa'i khrag khrig gis gang gis ming bris la//sngags pa khro ba dang bcas pas sngags bzlas na//de'i khrag 'gugs bar byed la//yid zhib dang 
54b5(rgyud): ldan pas sngags bzlas na//slar gtong bar 'gyuro//'khor le de nyid zla bas brgyan cing gsal ba//go ro tsa nas gro ga la skar ma rgyal gyi nyin bris te//bud med 
54b6(rgyud): gang gi rtag tu bcangs na//rig pa'i rgyal po de nyid de'i lhumasu 'jug par 'gyuro//bud med dang zhi ba'i 'dus pa'i kun tu spyod pa'i mi'i mchog de'i lag  
54b7(rgyud): mthil na yod pa'i dpal gang gis kyang 'phrog par mi nus la//rnam pa de lta bu'i   mchog dang ldan pas ni//dngos grub thams cad kyi 'bras bu ster bar 'gyuro//
54b8(rgyud): bcom ldan   'das 'di la ni//brtul zhugs dang spyod pa dang bsnyung ba dang cho ga med par yang //cho ga zhib mo'i sbyor ba'i tshul 'di nyid kyis//las thams cad ji 
55a1(rgyud): @#/ O /ltar 'dod pa bzhin byed do//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus bsam pa//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dam pa ste//'od zer tshogs ni gsal ba'i mdangs//
55a2(rgyud): 'khor lo'i phreng bas rnam par klubs//de dbus 'khor lo rtsibs brgyad dang //pad+ma'i phreng ba shin du mdzes//slar yang 'khor lo bri bar bya//rdo rje phreng ba rnam 
55a3(rgyud): par phro//sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje yi//dkyil 'khor dkyil 'khor lta bu yin/namakha' thams cad g.yo byed pa//rang gi lus la spro bar bya//dkar dang mar dang

55a5(rgyud): rol rdo rje phreng ba ni//'od zer 'bar ba'i phreng 'khrugs bri//'di ni bsrung ba chen mo ste/thams cad bslung ba bla na med/rim dang nang dang dug dang ni/mkha' 'gro ma yis 'tshe 
55a6(rgyud): ba dang //gdon dang bdung dang log 'dren dang //gang yang lus srul la segs pa//grib ma yang ni mi ('a)gom na/de'i lus lta smos ci dgos//zla gzugs lta bu'i yi ge lta//
55a7(rgyud): phyogs bcur rnam par 'byed par byed//dkar po yis ni zhi 'gyur te//nag po ska cig dag gis bsod//dmar po yis ni dbang byed do//de nyid kyis ni nges par 'gugs/ser 
55a8(rgyud): pos nges par 'ching 'gyur te//dmag gi dpung ni 'ching bar 'gyur//zhes bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs te//las thams cad 'byung ba zhes bya ba'i bsrung 'khor la'o//

55b2(rgyud): zla ba'i dkyil 'khor lta bu yi//dkyil 'khor dkyil 'khor lta bur bsam//bcu drug 'dab mas rnam par brgyan//de dbus pad chen 'dab brgyad pa//rdo rje rtse lnga pa'i ya mchog/

55b8(rgyud): 'dag ma du med la//blo dang ldan pas nyin bdun bsro//sgrub (pa)po yis brgya bzlas nas(ma)//rgyal po blon bcas 'dun na 'don//btsun mo'i 'khor dang bcas par ni//myur du 'gugs 
56a1(rgyud): @#/ O /par byed pa yin//gro ga yi ni 'dab ma la//ro yis sen rtsi ro rtsa nas//ma'i bdag po 'khor sa bcas pa//ming dang spel te bri bar bya//mngon par phyogs te

64'a3(rgyud): 'khor la sogs las gang ni//bcos ma'i sems kyis gang byed pa//phyi rol las rnams gsal byed pas//dong po'i las can rnams la bsngags//dngos grub de kun   
64'a4(rgyud): 'dir gnas pas//rgyal ba'i dngos nyid rig la min//gang yang sga shes bstan chos rnams//drang srong rnams kyis byas pa ltar//rang gi sems ni 'jug byed pa'i//
64'a5(rgyud): skyes bu'i bcos ma'i bya ba rnams//rgyud kyi rjes su 'brangs nas ni//'bad pa'i sdug bsngal slong bar byed//de phyir dkyil 'khor la sogs pa//bya  
64'a6(rgyud): ba'i cho ga la 'bad pa//thar pa dang bral phyi rol ste//skyes bu nyen mongs 'ba' zhig byed//mi rtog pas ni sangs mi rgya//rtog dang bcas pas kyang 
64'a7(rgyud): de bzhin//shin tu rnam dag yongs shes na//ling 'deng rnams ni de nyid 'gyur//rgyu dang 'bras bu'i rang bzhin ni///
64'b1(rgyud): dam pa nyid du yod min cing//rang bzhin gsal ba nyid kyi phyir//btang ba nyid kyang ma yin no//rang bzhin dbye ba thams 
64'b2(rgyud): cad kyi//rnam pa dag ni rnam gnas pa//rgya mtsho la ma rin chen bzhin//thog mtha' med pa'i tshul can yin//'jig rten 
64'b3(rgyud): rtag tu tha snyad spyod//'jig rten las 'das de dang 'gal//yong dang med ces bya ba ni//rang bzhin nyid du shes pa min//
64'b4(rgyud): 'di yi tshul gyis bar rtogs pa//rdzogs dang bral ba'i grol ba yin//rnal ma'i lam la bsten na ni//thams cad byas kyang 
64'b5(rgyud): rnam par grol//skyon dang yon tan 'phel byed pa'i//'khor ba dang ni mya ngan 'das//bde ba dang da ni 
64'b6(rgyud): sdug bsngal dngos//lhan cig skyes par yang dag gnas//mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba'i//
64'b7(rgyud): rdo rje mkha' 'gro bde ba'i mchog//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs//
64'b8(rgyud): rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dang la rdo rje mkha' 'gro las////
65a1(rgyud): @#/ O /dpa' bo gnyis med khyab pa ste/kun kyang 'dod pa'i gzugs can ma/yid gyi shugs kyis 'jug pa yin/de nas bcom ldan 'das la/
65a2(rgyud): lha mos slar yang mchod cing phyag byas te//'di skad ces gsol te//zhing skyong 'jigs pa chen po yin/ming dang zhing gi ming dang ni//gnas rnams kyi ni khyad par dang /gang 
65a3(rgyud): du bsgrub pa pos zos na//the tshom med par 'grub 'gyur ba/de dag thams cad mdor bsdus te//gtso bos bdag la bshad du gsol//de nas bcom ldan rdo rje can//
65a4(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs/nga yis gang mnga' ma bshad pa'i /gsang chen shin du rnyed bka' ba//gang yin khyed ni zhu 'dod pa/legs legs lha mo che//sgyu ma chen por
65a5(rgyud): rang 'gro ba/bshad kyis rtsa gcig yid kyis nyon//a ta ha se'i yul gnas pa//Izhi ba'i gdong zhes grags pa ste//rgyal po lha mo'i skye gnas byung //rdo rje yi ni  
65a6(rgyud): mtshon cha rnams//yul der gnas pa'i lha mo de//ka ta ma pa'i shing la brten/dris bu chen po zhes bya ba'i/khye tra pa la stobs po che/ko la kir ni stobs chen mo//gtsig+sa 
65a7(rgyud): pa'i skye gnas las byung ba/gtsigs pa'i gzugs can lha mo ste/rnam par 'gyur zhing 'jigasu rung//grong khyor der gnas drag shul can/ri yi rtse mor brten te gnas/
65a8(rgyud): me'i gdod pa zhes bya ba//k+ne ta pa la mdzes pa'i gdeng//de ra na rin bde byed ma//sme brtsegs chen po'i skye gnas 'byung//lha mo ga yi' mtshon cha bsnams//
65b1(rgyud): chos kyi dbang phyugasol ba mo//zhing der gnas par grags pa de//ta li' shing la brten te gnas//gyen du 'bar skra zhes bya ba//k+she Ta pa la gtsigs pa che//de'i ko tir rna 
65b2(rgyud): mo tshe//stobs po che'i skye gnas 'byung //lha mo lag na mdung can de//rnal 'byor dbang phyug kun gyi gachog/gnas der lha mo drag chen mo//ba ti' shing la rten te gnas//
65b3(rgyud): k+she Ta pa la lus chen po//mchog gi dbang phyug rgyu zhes bya//bi ra dzar ni phyi mo ste//ba dan gyi ni phyag rgya bsnams//zhi ba'i gzugs can lha mo che//'dod pa'i dbang phyug 
65b4(rgyud): rab brtsol ba//zhing der gnas par me 'bar ma//a bra'i shing la rten te gnas//ha har bgod pa sgra chen po//sems can thams cad 'jigs par bye//e ru dar 
65b5(rgyud): ni me lce'i kha//thod pa'i dge ba'i skye gnas 'byung //lha mo rdo rje shag ti bsnams//zhing der shin tu myos pas gnas//grong khor der gnas lha mo de//gser shing la ni 
65b6(rgyud): rten te gnas//dril bu sgra zhes bya ba yi//k+she tra pa la dpa' bo che//pre ta bu rir dmar ser te//'jigs pa po'i sbyan dang ldan//lha mo gtun shing mtshon cha bsnams//
65b7(rgyud): gatshigs pa'i skye gnas las 'byung ba'o//zhing der gnas pa'i lha mo der//dza ti' shing la rten te gnas//byin chen zhes bya' zhing skyong ni//shin tu gtsigs    
65b8(rgyud): shing 'jigasu rung //e lasu ring bong zhon ma//rab tu gtum pa'i skye gnas 'byung //phyag na zhags pa 'jigs chen mo//zhing der mos pas yang dganas//phyogs 
66a1(rgyud): rdo mkhro'  O /der gnas pa'i lha mo de//stobs chen pha rol gnon pa mo//glang chen rna ba zhes bya ba'i//zhing skyes mchog chen la skyes pa'o//kha ni yul du 
66a2(rgyud): ba glang rna//gtum ma'i skye gnas las byung ma//rdo rje lcags sgrog thogs nas ni//dgra rnams 'joms la mchog du brtson//gnas dor gnas pa'i lha mo der//
66a3(rgyud): ri'i rtse la rten te gnas//sbu gu'i byin pa can zhes bya'i//k+she Ta pa la 'jigs chen po//ma ru'i yul du rim pa mo//'jigs gdong dpa' mo'i skye gnas pa//
66a4(rgyud): lcags kyu zhags thogs lha mo de//zhing der shin tu myes pas gnas//yul der gnas pa'i lha mo de//dbye chen la ni brten te gnas//gtsigs pa zhes bya'i zhing 
66a5(rgyud): skyong ni/rmongs par byed pa bzhin yangs pa'o//na ga rar ni thong shol ma//sme brtsegs yan lag skye gnas byung//ro langal can ni 'od chen ma//dpa' bo kun 
66a6(rgyud): la dngos grub gsol//gnas der gnas pa'i bzang mo de//spu'i shing gi ldum rar gnas//spu yi byin pa zhes bya ba'i//k+she te pa la gatshigs pa che//bun da par 
66a7(rgyud): ti'i yul du ni//tsa mun tri zhes bya ba te//stogs po che'i skye gnas byung//kha T+wi ka yis lag pa brgyan//lha mo spyod dang grol bar byed//sdig pa ma lus bzad byed pa/
66a8(rgyud): bum pa zhes bya'i zhing skyong ni//zhing de na ni yang dag gnas//dza lan tar ni bzhin ding na//chags pa'i rigs kyi skyo gnas byung//rdo rje lcags sgrog thogs nas ni//bde 
66b1(rgyud): ba'i mchog ni kun la ster//kha grong khyer mchog der gnas lha mo/khar pa rtseg mar brten te gnas//ral pa gsum zhes bya ba yis//zhing skyong shin tu gtsigs pa che/
66b2(rgyud): bre ta pu rir glog gi gdong //gtsigs pa'i skye gnas yang dag byung /lha mo dbyig pa shag ti thogs//thams cad rdzogs byed ma//zhing der gnas pa'i lha mo de//
66b3(rgyud): drag po'i gzugs can 'jigs par byed/dril bu'i sga zhes bya ba yis//k+she tre pa la smad pa min//'ug pa'i gdong can 'jigasu rung/so pa rer ni me lce'i gdong//
66b4(rgyud): zangs gri yis ni lag pa brgyan//sha chen gyi ni brten le gnas//bde ba'i dbang phyug rab ster ba//zhing der gnas pa'i lhu chen mo//shal ma ri'i shing la gnas/
66b5(rgyud): bya ba kun mdzes zhes bya ba//k+she tra pa la 'jigs chen po//tsa ring ta'i yul du ni//ka ran dza gnas zhes bya ba//shag tig rtsigs pa'i skye gnas byung//shag ti 
66b6(rgyud): yi ni phyag rgya thogs//dril bu chen po zhes bya ba//k+she tra ba la stobs po che//sbrul dang ldan pa'i gyen skra can//bdug pa thams cad 'jigs par mdzad//dbu 
66b7(rgyud): brgyan du ni lha chen mo//gsang ba zhes bya ba'i skye gnas byung//lha mo rdo rje lcags sgrog thogs//shin tu grag shing gsal ba'i gzugs//sgra chen zhes bya drag pa'i 
66b8(rgyud): gzugs//k+she tra pa la stobs po che//gnas mchog der gnas lha mo de//a sho ka'i shing rten gnas//dza lan da rar gtum mo ni/zhi ba yi ni skye gnas byu//
67a1(rgyud): rngo mkhro' @#/ O /lag na zangs gri phyar te thogs//dbang phyug thams cad ster ba mo//gnas der gnas pa grags chen mo//gser shing la ni brten te gnas/
67a2(rgyud): bskyes pa zhes bya'i dpa' bo che//bdud rnams thams cad 'jigs par byed//k+she re kar ni skye bo'i ma//dag cing sngo la stogs chen ma//ral gri lag thogs lha mo che//dngos 
67a3(rgyud): grub thams cad gter ba mo//lha mo chen mo gyen skra 'bar//sa la'i shing la brten te gnas//lhun po chen po zhes bya ba'i//k+she tra pa la zhing der gnas//ma ya pu rer 'dod ma ste/
67a4(rgyud): la 'jigs (pa)'dong gi skye gnas byung//rdo rje shag ti dge ba thogs//sgrub pos rnams la stobs ster ba//gzugs sngo 'jigs pa chen po ste//'byung po'i shing la rten te gnas/
67a5(rgyud): der gnas 'jigs pa zhes bya ba'i//k+ke tra pa la stobs po che//su ta nar ni a bra ke/dgra rnams thams cad 'jigs byed pa//thong shol dang ni ga ya thegs//sems can 
67a6(rgyud): thams cad dbang phyug ma//du ru shing la brten te gnas//sems can thams cad 'jigs par byeng/brtul zhugs chen po zhes bya ba'i//k+She tra pa la zhing der gnas//rgyal po'i 
67a7(rgyud): khab tu dza na sa//khrag chen gyi ni skye gnas byung//gshin rje lcags bskyur yang dag ldan//pha rol g.yul las rgyal bar mdzad//zhing der gnas pa'i lha mo de//dzi la'i sgra 
67a8(rgyud): 'byin 'jigs par byed//rna chen zhes bya'i zhing skyong ni/rtag tu de dang nye bar gnas//bod yul du ni lhan skyes te//rang byung gi ni skye gnas byung//chu bsrin rgyal mtshan lag 
67b1(rgyud): na thogs//zhi zhing gsal ba'i gzugs can te//yul der gnas ba'i lha mo de//brag gi khrim la rten te gnas//long spyod zhes bya'i dpa' bo che//k+se tra pa la thub 
67b2(rgyud): dka' ba'o//ma la der ni dbang bskur ma//tho ba yis ni phyag rgya thogs//sgrub pa po la rtag tu rtse//grags dang dpal dang bkra shis ster//zhing der gnas    
67b3(rgyud): pa'i a lha mo de/mngar ba'i shing la brten te gnas//skyes bu'i dbang phyug zhes bya ba/zhing skyong sna tshogs dbang phyug yod/zhing rnams kyi ni bu mo dang//dpa' bo 
67b4(rgyud): rnams ni dngos grub ster//rtag tu mtshan mo 'bu bar byed/mkha' la spyod pa rnyed dka' ba'i//dngos grub yangs pa bster 'gyur te//sgrub pa po'i las rnam+sa
67b5(rgyud): ni;thams cad 'grub 'gyur the tshom med//de nas dngos grub kun ster   ba'i//mchod pa'i cho ga bshad par bya//me tog sdug pa de bzhin du//mar ('i)dri yi khyad   
67b6(rgyud): par dang//gtor ma'i gnod kyis nye bar mchod //sha rlon dang ni nya las sogs//bza' ba'i rdzas ni thams cad kyang//de bzhin 'dud rtsi lnga yi mchod//g.yon  
67b7(rgyud): gyi char ni snod yin te//g.yas su khro bo sten par bya//oM shing gcig dang dung khrod dang //ri bong dang ni ri sul phug/grong dang bzhi mdo zhing dang(ni)//khyad par 
67b8(rgyud): du ni khang stong dang//sems can snod dang thang dang ni//khyad par du ni chu la gnas//nag po drag po brag chen po//lha sbyin mnyam du bsten pa dang/nag po cigs
68a1(rgyud): @#/ O /pa 'jigs byed dang//dga' bas 'das dang log 'dren rnams//rgan byed ma dang drag mo dang//'jigs byed ma dang u ma dang/7rgyal dang rnam 
68a2(rgyud): par rgyal ma dang/mi thub bzhan (zi)gyis mi thub ma//nag mo bzang mo nag chen mo//nag sbom ma dang rnal 'byor ma//dbang po dang ni zla ba mo//drug du sdang dang 'phyang ba mo//
68a3(rgyud): 'phrog byed ma dang mar me ma//'bi byed grong gnas rnal 'byor ma//drag mo'i gzugs dang gzugs chen mo//mche ba tshigs dang thod chen ma//thod 'phreng phreng can kha (I)tang na/
68a4(rgyud): sbrul (I)rtsal gri bgra sta can//rdo rje de bzhin gzhu can ma/de nyid chen mo'i mkha' gro lnga//las kun rjes su bsgrub byed pa//sbyor ba'i dkyil 'khor rgyal chen mo//de bzhin rdo rje dbang

68a6(rgyud): ta na ba ba ban da na kha ga kha da na//sarba du sh+TanaM ha na ha g+ha ga ga ta ya ka mya shan hUM ting ku ru hUM hUM paT dzasahA//bdud rtsi yis ni kha bkang ste//lha rnams thams cad tshim par bya/
68a7(rgyud): rnal 'byor rnal 'byor ma yis ni//las rnams thams cad bsgrub par bsgom//oM bak+sha ba k+sha sarba ye ge shwa ri hUM hUM dz+wa swAhA//lasogs sbyor ba ji bzhin du//gtor ma'i las 
68a8(rgyud): ni sbyar bya ste//zhing dang rgyas dang dbang dang dgug//bsang dang sdang dang bcing ba la//bsgrub pos gtor ma de nyid kyis//las rnams thams cad bsgrub bya zhing//ye shes 
68b1(rgyud): /lcags kyu'i bye brag gis//rang 'dod lha ni tshim par bya//zhing dang gnas dang de bzhin grong //grong khyed rir yi rtse mo ru//'byung po thams cad tshogs gyur par//'dus nas sgrub 
68b2(rgyud): pos la mchog ster//gnas la sogs par rtag gnas shing//sgrub pa po ni gus ldan la//lha mo rnams kyis mchog ster par//sgrub pa po la the tshom med//ging gis 
68b3(rgyud): sgrub pa po 'grub pa'i//ro yi bsgrub pa bshad par bya//rgan me ma yin rma med cing//mdze dang shu ba la sogs spangs//shin du mnyam par bzhag pa yis//'byung po'i nyi ma 
68b4(rgyud): la bsgrub bya//bdud rtsi lnga yi khyad par dang//lha yi chu yis bkru bar bya//mar khu chen pos byug pa dang//bdug pa chen pos deb dug bya//sangs rgyas thams cad bdag la gson/
68b5(rgyud): zhes bya la sogs sngon bzhin brjod//ha hA ha hA hUM hUM he hA+e//zhes bya'i rgod pa rnam pa brgyad//bsgrub pa'i cho ga ji bzhin bya//me la gnas pa'i sa bon ni//rlung gis bon gyis phreg/sbyig 
68b6(rgyud): tsug tu ni sbyar byas la//sa yi steng du mchod par bya//rnal 'byor ma'i dbus gnas par//'bad pa yis ni myur du lta//bsgrub pa po bdag nyid bsam bya ba//skye gnas dbus su 
68b7(rgyud): rim bzhin blta//'bar 'phreng gi ni rgya mtsho ste//pho nya 'od dang bcas lta zhes//mkha' gro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba'i//rdo rje mkha' gro bde ba'i mchog/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs/
68b8(rgyud): bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs//rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje mkha' gro las/'du ba'i gnas nges pa dang//bsgrub pa'i cho ga'i rim par phye ba ste//bcwo brgyad pa'o//
69a1(rgyud): rdo mkhro' @#/ O //de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//ma mo dkyil 'khor dge ba dang //de bzhin pho nya'i khyad par dang/'byung po'i gdon gyi rig pa che//gang gzhan 
69a2(rgyud): lus srul la sogs pa//de bzhin ldags kyi phreng ba bsnams//de ni khyod la bshad kyis nyon//gru bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa//sgo skyong dang ni ldan pa yin//dkyil 'khor nyams dga' bri 
69a3(rgyud): bar bya//dngos grub kun gyi yon tan gnas//'dod yon lnga'i rab rgyas pa//mchod pa lnga dang yang dag ldan//ro la g.yogs pa'i ras sam ni//mtshon gyis rgyab pa'i sar dag dang//
69a4(rgyud): bu tsas pa'i ras dang ni//ba glang klo ba la sogs la//dam tshig can gyi 'dri mkha' gyi//sgrub po mchog gis 'di ru bzhug/tshes brgyad dang ni bcu bzhi la//'byung po'i dkyil 'khor sgrub 
69a5(rgyud): par bya//de nas 'bum phrag gcig bzlas pas//mngon sum du ni mdun gnas 'gyur//tshangs pa mo dang zhi ba mo//gzhon nu mdang phag mo dang /dbang mo dang ni tsa mun tra//tshogs 
69a6(rgyud): kyi dbang mo bsreg byed ma//rim pa gnyis pa'i 'o//dbang po gshin rje chu lha dang//lus dan dang ni yid btags gtso//bsreg byed bden bral rlung gi lha//rim pa gsum pa'i 'o//nyi ma 
69a7(rgyud): dang ni zla ba dang//mig dmar dang ni gza' lag dang//phur pu dang ni pab sangs//sben pa dang ni ra hu la//rim pa zhi pa'i 'o//tshangs pa khyab 'jig de bzhin du//ru tra bsked byed sgo bzhir ro//
69a8(rgyud): sa dang chu dang me dang rlung//'byung ba chen po'i khams rnams dang//mkha'agro ma dang mar me ma//'jib byed 'phrog byed btsas pa'i gtso//'byung po las kyang 'byung ba'i gzugs//bsgrub pos 
69b1(rgyud): cho ga bzhin du bsgrub//dkyil 'khor chon po'i sbyor ba yis//phyi rol dkyil 'khor bri bar bya//dbus su kun nas zlum por ni//ri mo mdzes par bskor bar bya//da pad+ma 'dab 
69b2(rgyud): rgyad bris nas ni//ge sar gsang ba'i sbyong yul gyi //dbus su khro bo chen po bri//zhal gcig phyag ni bzhi pa ste//zhal gyi che ba cung zad gtsigs //khro dang bcas shing bzheng pa'i 
69b3(rgyud): zhal/sku mdog ma sngon po drag chen po/thod pa'i phreng bas rnam par klubs//yid dgas la zhon nyi mar bzhugs//sum bcu gsum pa 'jig par mdzad//gar dang ldan   
69b4(rgyud): zhing stag lpags bsnams//sbyan bsum shin tu gsal ba'i gzugs//rdo rje 'bar ba ba dan gsum//khrag mchog gang ba'i shong dang bcas/mda' dang gzhu ni de bzhin te//
69b5(rgyud): g.yas dang g.yon gyi phyagi 'phyar//shar du mkha'agro ma bris te//lho yi phyogasu 'phrog byed ma//'jib byed lha mo nub tu bri//byang du lha mo 'bar ma'o//lha mo phag mo zhes bya ba//
69b6(rgyud): phyag bzhi yige hri las byung //sku ma(de)ga sngon mo drag chen mo/phag gi gdong can 'jigs par byed//mda' dang de bzhin gzhu dang ni/rgyal mtshan thong pa pa thogs pa'o//zhi ba mo 
69b7(rgyud): ni phem las byung/mdog dmar giza brjid chen mo ste//thod pa dang ni lcags kyu dang//kha tre 'dam bu de bzhin no//gzhon nu ma ni hi las byung/kha dog ser po phyag bzhi ba//
69b8(rgyud): mdud rtse gsum dang ba dan gsum//mda' dang gzhu ni de bzhinano//tsa mun tri ni ho las byung//mdog sngo lag pa bzhi pa ste//kha T+wAM dang ni thod pa dang//gshor dang de   
70a1(rgyud): rdo mkhro'  O /bzhin Da ma ru/bam las byung ba'i tshangs pa mo//mdog dkar lag pa bzhi pa ste//pad+ma dang ni thong pa dang //de bzhin (gzhan na re'u)gzha'o//tshogs kyi dbang mo 
70a2(rgyud): gam las byung//sngo sangs mdog ste lag bzhi ma//mdung rtse gsum dang thong pa dang//bgrang phreng spos kyi la du thogs//khyab 'jig mi ni bi las byung/mdog sngon lag pa bzhi 
70a3(rgyud): pa ste//chang dang chu yi thong pa dang//zhags pa kha T+waM de bzhin gzhan//dbang mo oM las byung ba ste//mdog ser lag pa bzhi dang ldan//rdo rje shag ti thong pa dang //sdig 'dzub dang ni zhags 
70a4(rgyud): pa nyid//thams cad gyen skra 'bar ba dang//sna tshogs kyis ni brgyan pa ste//ro yi gdan bzhugs sbyan sum dang//stag gis lbags pas rkeng dkris pa'o//shar sgor tshangs
70a5(rgyud): pa bri bya ste//mdog dgar lag pa bzhi dang ldan//thong pa dang ni bgrang phreng dang//ril ba lci lugs pad ma thogs//byang du de bzhin khyab 'jug ste//mdog sngo lag pa bzhi dang 
70a6(rgyud): ldan/dung dang de bzhin 'khor lo dang//thong pa dang ni dbyug pa'i mchog/lho ru ru tra bris byas te//du ba'i mdog can lag pa bzhi//rtse gsum mdung dang thod pa dang//
70a7(rgyud): zhags pa dang ni bgrang phreng thogs//nub du skye mchod bri bya ste//mdog ser lag pa pa bzhi dang ldan//shag ti dang ni dbyug pa dang//dpa' bo'i thod pa bgrang phreng thogs;

100b7(rgyud): pa ru/mar mo'i tshes brgyad bcu bzhil//pha mes kyi ni nags su gzugs//de ni 'bad te blangs nas su//du khrod du ni bla ma dang//he ru ka dpal mchod byas la//
100b8(rgyud): grub pa'i sa bon bzung byas te//thod pa ru ni bzhag pa la//cho ga bzhin du mngon bsngags su te//mgul par bcings na sbed par 'gyur//mnyam par bzhag te 
101a1(rgyud): @#/ O /bcing ba dgrol//bya rog nag po'i thod pa dang//mdung ngan mtshon gyis bsad pa yi//I bskyes bu yi ni zad pa ru//sbrul nag 
101a2(rgyud): po yi zhag gis ni//gser shing la sogs sa bon la//yang dang yang du bsgo byas la//grog mkhar gyi ni sa dag dang//dur khrod sa yis bsres bkang ste//bzhi mdo
101a3(rgyud): lam gyi dbus sam ni//chu bo yi ni 'gram dag tu//sgrub bos shin tu mnyam bzhag pas thod par dgongs mo'i dus su bzhag//gtor (ma)sbyin zhing bcu ba dang //bsrung 
101a4(rgyud): ba dag ni nyin re bya//kha T+wAMka la sogs pa yi//dpal ldan he ru ka gzugs bya//lag g.yon thod pa bzung nas ni//brtul zhugs spyod pa ldan pa yis//cho ga bzhin
